country_event = {
    id = 50
    title = "Enable Disable Dynamic Loc"
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		slots = { 
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = able_modifier
				OR = {
					has_province_modifier = mod_0
					NOT = {
						has_province_modifier = mod_1
					}
				}
			}
			secede_province = THIS
			state_scope = { change_region_name = "§R[Disabled]§!" }
			secede_province = QQQ
		}
		slots = { 
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = able_modifier
				has_province_modifier = mod_1
			}
			secede_province = THIS
			state_scope = { change_region_name = "§G[Enabled]§!" }
			secede_province = QQQ
		}
	}
	
	option = {
        name = "Enable Disable Dynamic Loc Complete"
    }
	
}

country_event = {
    id = 60
    title = "Force UTI to  Assimilate"
	is_triggered_only = yes

    immediate = {
		3635 = { secede_province = THIS }
		any_owned = { assimilate = yes }
		3635 = { secede_province = QQQ }
		primary_culture = noculture
	} 

    option = {
        name = "Woo"
    }
}
